ParlaMint-AT_2020-12-10-027-XXVII-NRSITZ-00069_d8e27767	Mr President. Mrs. Federal Minister! Dear ladies and gentlemen! The Government's proposal to amend the Chemicals Act focuses primarily on more effective ways to combat illegal trade and other violations of the regulation of fluorinated greenhouse gases and on contributing to the Austrian and Union-wide reduction plans in order to be able to implement them. FCKW are mainly used in air conditioning, cooling equipment and so on. Although they are present in much smaller quantities, they are much more dangerous than other greenhouse gases, such as methane or CO2. If one compares 2018 figures concerning the illegal import of CFCs into Europe and calculates this to CO2, that corresponds to a value of 16 million tonnes of imported CO2 or, in other words, to the emissions of four coal-fired or 4 million cars. In Austria, for example, 5 million cars are allowed. For this reason alone, this law is a very important step in the right direction. A second aspect is the improvement in the circular economy so that these products and materials can be recycled in the future and thus can also produce clean products, say products in which these chemicals are no longer used. One of the main components of this package of legislation is to make access to explosive raw materials more difficult by the introduction of an authorisation system. These raw materials, such as hydrochloric acid or hydrogen peroxide, are suitable for the production of explosives by simple means. Unfortunately, they are used again and again by criminals and terrorists. In the future, there will be a documentation requirement for companies providing raw materials to other companies. Suspected transactions, exits and theft shall be registered in a national contact point established by the Federal Criminal Office. – This is an essential area. When we remember that criminal and terrorist activities have increased worldwide, this part of the law is an essential step towards greater security for our people. If we take climate protection seriously, then we must do everything we can to use the existing biomass resources in Austria in a sustainable way.

The national leaders are given the opportunity to define a price band with minimum and upper limits. Therefore, the price can be determined flexibly according to objective criteria. That's the goal we're going to do. Where minimum or maximum charges or surcharges are fixed in the regulations of the countries depending on the length of the journey or the length of the journey, it may also be established that these indicators, namely journey duration and journey distance, are to be determined. It is important – I say this here in the House also for the interpretation of this innovation – to: The lower limit for the total price of pre-ordered journeys shall not be higher than the charges of the tariffs – should they be present in the countries. That is, I think, a very important area. You get in – you've pre-ordered, you know the price – and you don't need a taxameter. This is the principle in the pre-ordered journeys. The second point is also an important one: Other passengers may also be picked up from other places on pre-ordered journeys or transported to different destinations. That's what we call taxi sharing. Ladies and gentlemen, we are thus opening up a large window of opportunities: flexibility, ecology, efficiency, and cheap travel for passengers. [[Congratulation with Greens and ÖVP.]] I wish I could say that taxiinnung is fighting for everyone in the future and working for everyone. I hope that the industry will soon be good and that good business will be done. Because such misunderstandings still exist in the debate, I would like to clear them again. There are uniform rules, taxis and rental cars are combined into a fully uniform trade from 1.1.2021 onwards. The same rules apply to all except the same rules. All drivers need a taxi ticket, all have the same training, all have to meet the same quality standards, all are subject to the same rules for travel prices, all are subject to the same rules if they want to offer pre-ordered journeys, all are subject to the same rules if they want to drive with taxameter. There's no difference. We give the countries better opportunities to do exactly what they call for, namely, for pre-ordered journeys, price bands, which are based on objective criteria such as likely journey duration and distance. We are opening them up exactly this opportunity. We have been able to eliminate very, very many misunderstandings in the last few weeks, so today, after intensive debates, we are launching a novel that will enable us to make innovative and digital offers possible, but also give security and improve the ability to act in relation to the previous decision-making process for the federal states. [[Congratulation with Greens and ÖVP.]] A keyword to the second government template, amendment of the STVO: This is an extension on the one hand. Colleagues Schroll takes them too long as I've heard. But today we cannot estimate how long we will have this exceptional situation. There is the possibility to remove weekend and holiday bans. That doesn't mean we have to make use of it. I would also like to stress once again in this context: We observe the situation very closely. There is currently no need for this, but I would like to be able to make it possible to travel home if the handlemen are back on borders. This novel is also used for this purpose. Thank you very much.
